## 2026-06-04 — INCEPTION PRE-MARKET

### Market posture

**Overall: Risk-on, AI supercycle in full swing. Pro-cyclical bias is correct.**

- **SPY**: ~754 (June 3 close), at/near highs. Uptrend intact.
- **Fed**: Holding at 3.5–3.75%; sticky CPI at 3.8% means no cuts in 2026. JPM projects a 25bp
  hike in Q3 2027. Higher-for-longer is a mild multiple headwind but AI earnings growth is
  dominating.
- **Earnings**: S&P 500 earnings up ~28% YoY; median beat rate at 4-year high (+6% median
  surprise Q1 2026). Semis leading.
- **Sectors leading**: Semis, Energy, Defense, Industrials. Consumer Discretionary and commercial
  RE lagging (consumer strained by high rates + negative real wages).
- **AI capex**: AMZN $200B, GOOGL $175-185B, MSFT $190B, META $115-135B for calendar 2026.
  This is the largest sustained capex super-cycle in history. Beneficiaries are clear.
- **Geopolitical risk**: Middle East tensions keeping oil elevated → inflation stickiness.
- **VIX**: Not elevated (market near highs, complacency-zone). Risk-on.

### Key holdings/candidates research

**NVDA (Nvidia) — Tier 1 core**
- Q1 FY2027 results (reported May 20, 2026): revenue $81.62B (+85% YoY), EPS $1.98 vs $1.76
  est. Massive beat. Q2 FY2027 guidance: $91B ± 2%. Blackwell architecture scaling.
- Current pre-market price: ~$212–213 (daily close June 3: $214.86).
- Analyst consensus: Strong Buy, avg target ~$296 (+37% upside). 61 analysts polled.
- Thesis: AI GPU monopoly. Hyperscalers can't build AI clusters without H100/H200/Blackwell.
  Revenue guide $91B next quarter means full-year FY2027 run rate ~$350B+. At ~$213/share,
  this is the highest-conviction AI infrastructure name. Every dollar of hyperscaler capex
  ultimately flows through NVDA in some form.
- Risk: Concentration in one architecture; AMZN Trainium and Google TPUs are partial
  substitutes but still small fraction of total AI compute.
- Entry: ~$213 at market open (or market price). Size: 22% (~USD 22,000).

**AVGO (Broadcom) — Tier 1, post-earnings dip**
- Q2 FY2026 results (reported June 3, 2026): Revenue $22.187B (+48% YoY); Non-GAAP EPS $2.44;
  **AI semiconductor revenue $10.8B (+143% YoY)**. Q3 AI guide: ~$16B (~+200% YoY).
- Stock dropped ~14-15% overnight to ~$408 pre-market (from $478.62 June 3 close).
  **Reason for drop: infrastructure software revenue slightly below whisper, and AI chip
  full-year forecast not raised above the prior $100B+ FY2027 guide.**
- This is a NON-THESIS dip. The AI acceleration story is not just intact — it's accelerating
  faster than any prior guidance (tripling to $16B in a single quarter). Software is a smaller,
  slower segment. HSBC raised target to $600 (from $450).
- Pre-market bid/ask ~$460/$500 (wide spread, thin liquidity). Actual open price likely
  $420–460. Will buy notional at whatever price opens.
- Thesis: Custom ASIC + networking leader. 6 major hyperscaler custom chip customers. Every
  AI inference chip not made by NVDA increasingly runs through AVGO custom silicon. FY2027
  AI revenue guide $100B+ (HSBC: $100.2B estimate). At $430-460, stock is trading at a
  fraction of its AI revenue potential.
